📡 Radar Stocks

Every stock any existing source has surfaced — Watchlist, Heat Map, and LSE Screener — merged once per stock. A discovery list, not a recommendation: this shows the same evidence already computed elsewhere, ranked by existing Signal Quality so the clearest-agreeing evidence appears first, without ranking, scoring, or calling anything a "buy" or "opportunity."

Discovery sources (find NEW stocks): Watchlist, Heat Map, LSE Screener (Volume/Gainers/Losers), and market-wide Broker Research (rating/target changes across the whole LSE, not just watchlist stocks). Evidence sources (enrich stocks already discovered, but cannot discover a stock on their own): Google/Yahoo/Reuters/Bloomberg/FT news feeds and the existing AI Evidence Review — these are per-ticker lookups, not a whole-market scan, so they can only add evidence to a stock some other source already surfaced. Not available, shown honestly rather than invented: genuine insider buy/sell transaction data (only static insider ownership % exists, which is never presented as trading activity) and any web source beyond the news feeds listed above.

📊 Signal Backtest (Technical Signals Only)

Read this before the numbers below:
  • Survivorship bias — this universe reflects TODAY's FTSE 100/250 membership only; stocks that were delisted, acquired, or relegated during the tested period are invisible to this test.
  • No historical News/Broker/AI Evidence data — this backtest covers ONLY the Technical/Market dimensions and the RSI-overextension component of RISK. It says nothing about whether NEWS, BROKER, or AI Evidence Review signals have ever worked.
  • Pre-cost, pre-slippage — returns shown are raw price moves, not what a real trade would net.
  • Price returns, not total return — not adjusted for dividends.
  • Data revisions — historical prices can occasionally be revised by the data source after the fact; this cannot be fully corrected for with a free data source.
  • Small samples are flagged, not hidden — any result built from fewer than 30 events is marked "insufficient data," not reported as a finding.
  • Multiple-testing corrected — with many signals and horizons tested together, apparent "significance" is checked against a Bonferroni-corrected threshold, not the usual 0.05.
  • Correlation, not causation — even a result that clears every bar above only shows a historical association, never a guarantee, explanation, or prediction of future performance.
